- **review-animations**: Reviews animation and motion code — web (CSS, WAAPI, Motion/Framer Motion) and React Native (Reanimated, Gesture Handler, Animated, Moti) — against a high craft bar derived from Emil Kowalski's design engineering philosophy. Use when reviewing a diff, PR or component that adds or changes transitions, animations, gestures, springs, or press/haptic feedback. Default to flagging; approval is earned.
